   359	
   360	  // Page-size change recompute. Per spec §localStorage robustness:
   361	  // "Changing the page size recomputes the current page so the first
   362	  // visible row stays visible if possible." If the user is on page 2
   363	  // with pageSize 50 (rows 50-99) and switches to pageSize 100, the
   364	  // new pageIndex must be 0 (rows 0-99 — keeps row 50 visible);
   365	  // switching back to pageSize 50 from there returns them to page 1
   366	  // (rows 50-99). The math is: firstVisibleRow = pageIndex * oldSize;
   367	  // newPageIndex = floor(firstVisibleRow / newSize).
   368	  useEffect(() => {
   369	    const oldSize = prevPageSizeRef.current;
   370	    const newSize = filters.pageSize;
   371	    if (oldSize !== newSize) {
   372	      // Use a functional setState so we read the freshest pageIndex
   373	      // (not a closure-captured one).
   374	      setPageIndex((prev) => Math.floor((prev * oldSize) / newSize));
   375	      prevPageSizeRef.current = newSize;
   376	    }
   377	  }, [filters.pageSize]);
   378	
   379	  // Visible importable set under the CURRENT filter window. The
   380	  // click-time intersection in `handleImport` reads from this same set
   381	  // so the POST body never contains a row hidden by filter. NOTE:
   382	  // intentionally NOT scoped to `pageRows` — pagination must NOT
   383	  // narrow the bulk-select target (per spec §Pagination vs
   384	  // virtualization risk: cross-page selection accumulated through
   385	  // "Select all importable in current filter" must still ship).
   386	  const visibleImportableInFilter = useMemo(() => {
   387	    const set = new Set<string>();
   388	    for (const row of filteredRows) {
   389	      if (isImportable(row) && row.sourceSessionKey !== null) {
   390	        set.add(row.sourceSessionKey);
   391	      }
   392	    }
   393	    return set;
   394	  }, [filteredRows]);
   395	
   396	  // Visible importable set across the FULL merged set (i.e. ignoring the
   397	  // filter window). Used to compute the "+K hidden by filters" caption:
   398	  // a key is "hidden by filter" when it's in `selected`, importable in
   399	  // the full merged set, but NOT importable in the current filter
   400	  // window.
   401	  const visibleImportableInMerged = useMemo(() => {
   402	    const set = new Set<string>();
   403	    for (const row of mergedRows) {
   404	      if (isImportable(row) && row.sourceSessionKey !== null) {
   405	        set.add(row.sourceSessionKey);
   406	      }
   407	    }
   408	    return set;
   409	  }, [mergedRows]);
   410	
   411	  const handleToggleAll = useCallback(() => {
   412	    setSelected((prev) => {
   413	      // Toggle bulk-select against the CURRENT FILTER window. A user
   414	      // who narrows the view and clicks the header checkbox expects to
   415	      // select only what they can see (per spec § Bulk-select
   416	      // affordances: "Select all importable in current filter"). This
   417	      // is NOT scoped to the current page — the cross-page selection
   418	      // is the whole point of the affordance.
   419	      const importableKeys = Array.from(visibleImportableInFilter);
   420	      const allSelected =
   421	        importableKeys.length > 0 &&
   422	        importableKeys.every((k) => prev.has(k));
   423	      if (allSelected) {
   424	        // Clear only the keys that came from the current filter
   425	        // window; selection from outside the filter (the +K hidden
   426	        // by filters) is left alone — Clear hidden / Clear selection
   427	        // are the dedicated affordances for those.
   428	        const next = new Set(prev);
   429	        for (const k of importableKeys) next.delete(k);
   430	        return next;
   431	      }
   432	      const next = new Set(prev);
   433	      for (const k of importableKeys) next.add(k);
   434	      return next;
   435	    });
   436	  }, [visibleImportableInFilter]);
   437	
   438	  // Refs for the latest handleRescan / handleImport. Toast `onRetry`
   439	  // closures capture these refs (NOT the handlers directly), so a
   440	  // Retry click always invokes the LATEST handler — which re-derives
   441	  // the click-time intersection from current state, not from the
   442	  // state that was live when the failure-toast was first pushed.
   443	  // Without this, a Retry click after a rescan-between-attempts
   444	  // would read the pre-rescan `selected` / `sourceState` snapshot.
   445	  const handleRescanRef = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.resolve());
   446	  const handleImportRef = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.resolve());
   447	
   448	  const handleRescan = useCallback(async () => {
   449	    setPending("rescan");
   450	    try {
   451	      const report: RescanReport = await triggerRescan();
   452	      // Persist the success timestamp + push to local state so the
   453	      // ActionBar caption updates immediately. Per spec, the caption
   454	      // is explicitly scoped to "this browser" so we ONLY write on
   455	      // the success path; an error keeps the previous timestamp.
   456	      const iso = new Date().toISOString();
   457	      writeLastRescan(iso);
   458	      setLastRescanAt(iso);
   459	      pushToast({
   460	        kind: "success",
   461	        title: "Rescan complete",
   462	        message: rescanSummary(report),
   463	        details: rescanCounts(report),
   464	      });
   465	      await refetchAll();
   466	    } catch (error) {
   467	      pushToast({
   468	        kind: "error",
   469	        title: "Rescan failed",
   470	        message: messageFor(error),
   471	        onRetry: () => {
   472	          // Re-derive the rescan call at click time via the ref so
   473	          // we always invoke the LATEST handleRescan — not the
   474	          // closure-captured one from the render that pushed this
   475	          // toast. Fire-and-forget; the inner handler will push its
   476	          // own success/error toast.
   477	          void handleRescanRef.current();
   478	        },
   479	      });
   480	    } finally {
   481	      setPending(null);
   482	    }
   483	  }, [refetchAll, pushToast]);
   484	
   485	  const handleImport = useCallback(async () => {
   486	    setPending("import");
   487	    // Derive the import payload at click time from the currently-
   488	    // FILTERED rows that are still importable. Three defenses bake
   489	    // into one expression:
   490	    //   (a) F2 visible-intersection: a rescan that has just pruned a
   491	    //       row from the source list won't ship its key, even if the
   492	    //       reconciliation `useEffect` has not yet flushed.
   493	    //   (b) M2 importability: a row that has ALWAYS had a non-null
   494	    //       `sourceSessionKey` but is `up_to_date` (i.e. visible but
   495	    //       not eligible for import) won't ship either, even if the
   496	    //       UI somehow leaked a stale identity into `selected`.
   497	    //   (c) M3 filter-window: a row that the user selected, then
   498	    //       hid via a filter mutation, will NOT ship — only rows in
   499	    //       the current filter window are eligible.
   500	    //   (d) M5 cross-page: pagination does NOT narrow the eligible
   501	    //       set; a row selected on page 1 but currently displayed on
   502	    //       page 2 still ships if it remains in the filter window.
   503	    //       The filter pipeline is the gate, not pagination.
   504	    // The same `isImportable` helper used by SessionsTable governs
   505	    // (a)+(b), and the filter pipeline owns (c)+(d), so the rendered
   506	    // checkbox set, the action-bar count, and the POST body cannot
   507	    // drift apart.
   508	    //
   509	    // Build the visible-importable set fresh inside the handler so
   510	    // we read whatever state landed up to the moment of the click,
   511	    // not a value captured by `useMemo` at the previous render. This
   512	    // mirrors the F2 pattern where the click happens during a
   513	    // commit-time scheduler race; a stale memoized closure here
   514	    // would re-open the bug.
   515	    const src = sourceState.kind === "ok" ? sourceState.data : [];
   516	    const stor = storedState.kind === "ok" ? storedState.data : [];
   517	    const liveMerged = mergeSessions(src, stor);
   518	    const liveFiltered = applySort(
   519	      applyFilters(liveMerged, filters),
   520	      filters.sort,
   521	    );
   522	    const liveVisibleImportable = new Set<string>();
   523	    for (const row of liveFiltered) {
   524	      if (isImportable(row) && row.sourceSessionKey !== null) {
   525	        liveVisibleImportable.add(row.sourceSessionKey);
   526	      }
   527	    }
   528	    const keysToImport = Array.from(selected).filter((k) =>
   529	      liveVisibleImportable.has(k),
   530	    );
   531	    try {
   532	      const report: ImportReport = await importSourceSessions(keysToImport);
   533	      setSelected(new Set());
   534	      pushToast({
   535	        kind: "success",
   536	        title: "Import complete",
   537	        message: importSummary(report),
   538	        details: importCounts(report),
   539	      });
   540	      await refetchAll();
   541	    } catch (error) {
   542	      pushToast({
   543	        kind: "error",
   544	        title: "Import failed",
   545	        message: messageFor(error),
   546	        onRetry: () => {
   547	          // Per spec: "Error/retry: if Import fails and the user
   548	          // retries, the retry re-derives the effective selection at
   549	          // click time — if a rescan fired between attempts, the
   550	          // payload reflects the new state, never the pre-error
   551	          // cache." We dispatch through the ref so the click invokes
   552	          // the LATEST handleImport — its useCallback deps include
   553	          // `selected`, `sourceState`, `storedState`, `filters`, so
   554	          // the function identity changes whenever any of those does.
   555	          // Without the ref hop, this closure would capture the
   556	          // handler from the render that pushed the toast and would
   557	          // re-derive against THAT handler's stale closure scope.
   558	          void handleImportRef.current();
   559	        },
   560	      });
   561	    } finally {
   562	      setPending(null);
   563	    }
   564	  }, [selected, sourceState, storedState, filters, refetchAll, pushToast]);
   565	
   566	  // Keep the refs pointed at the LATEST handler identities so toast
   567	  // Retry closures (pushed earlier) always re-invoke the current
   568	  // versions — see comment on `handleRescanRef` / `handleImportRef`
   569	  // above for the rationale.
   570	  useEffect(() => {
   571	    handleRescanRef.current = handleRescan;
   572	  }, [handleRescan]);
   573	  useEffect(() => {
   574	    handleImportRef.current = handleImport;
   575	  }, [handleImport]);
   576	
   577	  // Selected count for the action bar mirrors the same intersection
   578	  // the import POST will use, so "Import selected (N)" always names
   579	  // the exact set that would ship if clicked now.
   580	  let selectedCount = 0;
   581	  for (const k of selected) {
   582	    if (visibleImportableInFilter.has(k)) selectedCount += 1;
   583	  }
   584	
   585	  // "+K hidden by filters" caption: keys the user has selected that
   586	  // are still importable in the merged set but NOT in the filter
   587	  // window. These are rows that fell out of the user's current view
   588	  // because of a filter, not because the backend pruned them.
   589	  let hiddenByFilterCount = 0;
   590	  for (const k of selected) {
   591	    if (
   592	      visibleImportableInMerged.has(k) &&
   593	      !visibleImportableInFilter.has(k)
   594	    ) {
   595	      hiddenByFilterCount += 1;
   596	    }
   597	  }
   598	
   599	  const onClearHidden = useCallback(() => {
   600	    setSelected((prev) => {
   601	      const next = new Set<string>();
   602	      for (const k of prev) {
   603	        if (visibleImportableInFilter.has(k)) next.add(k);
   604	      }
   605	      return next;
   606	    });
   607	  }, [visibleImportableInFilter]);
   608	
   609	  const onClearSelection = useCallback(() => {
   610	    setSelected(new Set());
   611	  }, []);
   612	
   613	  const onChangePage = useCallback((next: number) => {
   614	    setPageIndex(next);
   615	  }, []);
   616	
   617	  const onChangePageSize = useCallback(
   618	    (size: PageSize) => {
   619	      setFilter("pageSize", size);
   620	    },

   620	    },
   621	    [setFilter],
   622	  );
   623	
   624	  // M1a: row-click → URL-synced selection. The Phase-4 row-click →
   625	  // drawer flow ALSO fires on the same click (preserved in M1a;
   626	  // M1b retires it); the selection setter here only controls the
   627	  // right pane + URL. Narrow viewports also flip narrowMode to
   628	  // "session" so the user sees the session pane after the click.
   629	  const handleSelectRow = useCallback(
   630	    (rowKey: string) => {
   631	      selectRow(rowKey);
   632	      setNarrowMode("session");
   633	    },
   634	    [selectRow],
   635	  );
   636	
   637	  // M1a: "← Back to list" gesture. Per Resolved Decision #17 (spec
   638	  // line 1162) this only sets `narrowMode = "list"` — does NOT
   639	  // clear `selectedRowKey` and does NOT clear the URL. Esc is the
   640	  // distinct gesture for fully clearing.
   641	  const handleBackToList = useCallback(() => {
   642	    setNarrowMode("list");
   643	  }, []);
   644	
   645	  // M1a: Try Rescan from the session_not_found state. Fires the
   646	  // standard refetch — does NOT clear the URL (preserves the deep
   647	  // link in case Rescan finds the row).
   648	  const handleTryRescan = useCallback(() => {
   649	    void refetchAll();
   650	  }, [refetchAll]);
   651	
   652	  // M1a: Clear selection from the session_not_found state.
   653	  // Identical to Esc's effect on the URL: clears selectedRowKey
   654	  // and removes the `?session=` query param. Does NOT clear
   655	  // narrowMode (the user is on a narrow viewport ⇒ already
   656	  // visible-mode "session" but now empty; we flip to "list" so they
   657	  // see the list).
   658	  const handleClearSelectionFromNotFound = useCallback(() => {
   659	    selectRow(null);
   660	    setNarrowMode("list");
   661	  }, [selectRow]);
   662	
   663	  // M1a: global `Esc` handler. Clears the URL `?session=` slice +
   664	  // selectedRowKey + narrowMode in a single keystroke. Per spec
   665	  // line 567 + Resolved Decision #17, the handler is SCOPED:
   666	  // ignored when focus is in `<input>`, `<textarea>`,
   667	  // `[contenteditable]`, or any `[role="combobox"]`. This is so the
   668	  // Phase-4 SessionFilters search input still consumes Esc to
   669	  // clear its own value (the existing UX); only Esc events that
   670	  // bubble out of non-editable surfaces reach this handler.
   671	  //
   672	  // Identical behavior across narrow + wide viewports — on narrow
   673	  // it ALSO resets narrowMode to "list" (no session pane to be in).
   674	  useEffect(() => {
   675	    function isEditableTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
   676	      if (target === null) return false;
   677	      const el = target as HTMLElement;
   678	      // Tag check: input / textarea — both directly consume Esc
   679	      // semantics in their default behavior.
   680	      const tag = (el.tagName ?? "").toUpperCase();
   681	      if (tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA") return true;
   682	      // contenteditable elements (rare in this app, but the spec
   683	      // calls them out so M2 surfaces such as inline-edit notes
   684	      // would already be covered).
   685	      if (
   686	        typeof el.closest === "function" &&
   687	        el.closest('[contenteditable="true"]') !== null
   688	      ) {
   689	        return true;
   690	      }
   691	      // ARIA combobox role — covers e.g. the future-typeahead
   692	      // autocomplete patterns.
   693	      if (
   694	        typeof el.closest === "function" &&
   695	        el.closest('[role="combobox"]') !== null
   696	      ) {
   697	        return true;
   698	      }
   699	      return false;
   700	    }
   701	    function onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
   702	      if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
   703	      if (isEditableTarget(event.target)) return;
   704	      // setSelectedRowKey(null) + URL clear + narrowMode reset.
   705	      // selectRow handles the first two; setNarrowMode handles the
   706	      // third. All three are idempotent so calling them when there
   707	      // is nothing to clear is a no-op.
   708	      selectRow(null);
   709	      setNarrowMode("list");
   710	    }
   711	    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
   712	    return () => {
   713	      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
   714	    };
   715	  }, [selectRow]);
   716	
   717	  // M1a: derive the right-pane state. The four-state machine
   718	  // (per design.md §4.1) maps the (selectedRowKey, mergedRows,
   719	  // panel-state) tuple to one of:
   720	  //   - empty               → no selection
   721	  //   - loading             → selection set; row not yet merged;
   722	  //                           any GET still in flight
   723	  //   - ready-placeholder   → selection set; row IS in merged set
   724	  //                           (M1a placeholder until M2's tabs)
   725	  //   - session_not_found   → selection set; row NOT in merged set;
   726	  //                           ALL three GETs settled (per spec line
   727	  //                           572 — the "all three settled" gate is
   728	  //                           load-bearing because resolving "missing
   729	  //                           row" too early would flash spurious
   730	  //                           copy while fetches are still in flight)
   731	  // Note: `buildUrl` is referenced just to keep it in the import
   732	  // graph for future call sites (Esc + selectRow already use it via
   733	  // the hook).
   734	  void buildUrl; // see comment above
   735	  let sessionViewState:
   736	    | "empty"
   737	    | "loading"
   738	    | "ready-placeholder"
   739	    | "session_not_found";
   740	  if (selectedRowKey === null) {
   741	    sessionViewState = "empty";
   742	  } else {
   743	    const matchedRow = mergedRows.find((r) => r.rowKey === selectedRowKey);
   744	    if (matchedRow !== undefined) {
   745	      sessionViewState = "ready-placeholder";
   746	    } else if (
   747	      sourceState.kind === "loading" ||
   748	      storedState.kind === "loading" ||
   749	      errorsState.kind === "loading"
   750	    ) {
   751	      // While any of the three GETs is still in flight, render the
   752	      // generic "loading" copy. Resolving "missing row" too early
   753	      // would flash session_not_found while the merged list is
   754	      // still settling.
   755	      sessionViewState = "loading";
   756	    } else {
   757	      sessionViewState = "session_not_found";
   758	    }
   759	  }
   760	
   761	  // M1a: detect the narrow viewport so we can decide whether the
   762	  // "← Back to list" affordance should render. The CSS-driven
   763	  // `display: none` rules already hide the wrong pane below 900 px;
   764	  // this gate just keeps the back-to-list button DOM-present only
   765	  // on narrow + session mode (avoids a wide-viewport user finding
   766	  // it via screen reader or aria-* tooling).
   767	  const [isNarrow, setIsNarrow] = useState<boolean>(() => {
   768	    if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
   769	    if (typeof window.matchMedia !== "function") return false;
   770	    return window.matchMedia("(max-width: 899.98px)").matches;
   771	  });
   772	  useEffect(() => {
   773	    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
   774	    if (typeof window.matchMedia !== "function") return;
   775	    const mq = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 899.98px)");
   776	    const onChange = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => setIsNarrow(e.matches);
   777	    // addEventListener is the modern API; addListener is the legacy
   778	    // fallback. happy-dom + Chromium ship the former.
   779	    if (typeof mq.addEventListener === "function") {
   780	      mq.addEventListener("change", onChange);
   781	    } else if (typeof (mq as unknown as { addListener?: typeof onChange }).addListener === "function") {
   782	      (mq as unknown as { addListener: (l: typeof onChange) => void }).addListener(onChange);
   783	    }
   784	    return () => {
   785	      if (typeof mq.removeEventListener === "function") {
   786	        mq.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
   787	      } else if (
   788	        typeof (mq as unknown as { removeListener?: typeof onChange }).removeListener === "function"
   789	      ) {
   790	        (mq as unknown as { removeListener: (l: typeof onChange) => void }).removeListener(onChange);
   791	      }
   792	    };
   793	  }, []);
   794	  const showBackToList = isNarrow && narrowMode === "session";
   795	
   796	  return (
   797	    <>
   798	      {/* M1a: page title + scan-errors callout sit above the split-
   799	          pane <main>. They are NOT inside .list-pane / .session-pane
   800	          because they apply to the whole inspection surface, not a
   801	          single side. The toast queue stays a sibling of <main>
   802	          (Phase-4 pattern preserved). */}
   803	      <h1>Distill Portal</h1>
   804	      <main className="split-pane" data-narrow-mode={narrowMode}>
   805	        {/* List pane: <aside aria-label="Sessions list"> per design
   806	            §3.3. Phase-4 list chrome (filter strip, 8-column table,
   807	            pagination, action bar) renders inside this landmark
   808	            unchanged in M1a. M1b will compress the table to 4
   809	            columns and relocate Pagination + ActionBar into a sticky
   810	            list-panel footer; M1a leaves them. */}
   811	        <aside className="list-pane" aria-label="Sessions list">
   812	          <section className="panel">
   813	            <h2>Sessions</h2>
   814	            <ActionBar
   815	              selectedCount={selectedCount}
   816	              hiddenByFilterCount={hiddenByFilterCount}
   817	              pending={pending}
   818	              onRescan={handleRescan}
   819	              onImport={handleImport}
   820	              onClearHidden={onClearHidden}
   821	              onClearSelection={onClearSelection}
   822	              lastRescanAt={lastRescanAt}
   823	              now={now}
   824	            />
   825	            <SessionsView
   826	              sourceState={sourceState}
   827	              storedState={storedState}
   828	              mergedRows={mergedRows}
   829	              filteredRows={filteredRows}
   830	              pageRows={pageRows}
   831	              pageIndex={clampedPageIndex}
   832	              pageSize={filters.pageSize}
   833	              onChangePage={onChangePage}
   834	              onChangePageSize={onChangePageSize}
   835	              filters={filters}
   836	              projects={projects}
   837	              setFilter={setFilter}
   838	              setImportableOnly={setImportableOnly}
   839	              resetAll={resetAll}
   840	              selected={selected}
   841	              onToggle={handleToggle}
   842	              onToggleAll={handleToggleAll}
   843	              onRetry={() => {
   844	                void refetchAll();
   845	              }}
   846	              onRescan={handleRescan}
   847	              rescanPending={pending === "rescan"}
   848	              now={now}
   849	              selectedRowKey={selectedRowKey}
   850	              onSelectRow={handleSelectRow}
   851	              pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey={pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey}
   852	              onDeepLinkPulseEnd={onDeepLinkPulseEnd}
   853	            />
   854	            {errorsState.kind === "error" ? (
   855	              <p role="alert">
   856	                Failed to load scan errors: {errorsState.message}{" "}
   857	                <button type="button" onClick={() => void refetchAll()}>
   858	                  Retry
   859	                </button>
   860	              </p>
   861	            ) : (
   862	              <ScanErrorsCallout
   863	                errors={errorsState.kind === "ok" ? errorsState.data : []}
   864	              />
   865	            )}
   866	          </section>
   867	        </aside>
   868	        {/* Session pane: <article> per spec line 1121 / design §3.4.
   869	            M1a renders only the placeholder state machine; M2 will
   870	            mount the four-tab Tabs primitive inside this same
   871	            element. */}
   872	        <SessionView
   873	          state={sessionViewState}
   874	          showBackToList={showBackToList}
   875	          onBackToList={handleBackToList}
   876	          onClearSelection={handleClearSelectionFromNotFound}
   877	          onTryRescan={handleTryRescan}
   878	        />
   879	      </main>
   880	      {/* Toast queue lives outside <main> for DOM placement (it's a
   881	          fixed-position overlay anchored bottom-right via CSS;
   882	          pointer-events: none on the queue + auto on individual
   883	          toasts so the gap between toasts doesn't intercept clicks
   884	          on the page underneath). Sibling placement is purely a
   885	          layout choice — push/dismiss still re-renders App's JSX
   886	          walk because `useToastQueue` is App-level state; the
   887	          memoized merge/filter/sort/paginate chain is what keeps
   888	          the table render cheap. */}
   889	      <div className="toast-queue" role="region" aria-label="Notifications">
   890	        {toasts.map((toast) => (
   891	          <Toast
   892	            key={toast.id}
   893	            id={toast.id}
   894	            kind={toast.kind}
   895	            title={toast.title}
   896	            message={toast.message}
   897	            onRetry={toast.onRetry}
   898	            onDismiss={dismissToast}
   899	            details={toast.details}
   900	          />
   901	        ))}
   902	      </div>
   903	    </>
   904	  );
   905	}
   906	
   907	function toPanelState<T>(
   908	  result: PromiseSettledResult<T>,
   909	): PanelState<T> {
   910	  if (result.status === "fulfilled") {
   911	    return { kind: "ok", data: result.value };
   912	  }
   913	  if (isAbortError(result.reason)) {
   914	    return { kind: "loading" };
   915	  }
   916	  return { kind: "error", message: messageFor(result.reason) };
   917	}
   918	
   919	function isAbortError(error: unknown): boolean {
   920	  return error instanceof DOMException && error.name === "AbortError";
   921	}
   922	
   923	function messageFor(error: unknown): string {
   924	  if (error instanceof ApiError) {
   925	    return error.message;
   926	  }
   927	  if (error instanceof Error) {
   928	    return error.message;
   929	  }
   930	  return String(error);
   931	}
   932	
   933	// Plain-language summary line for a successful rescan, per spec
   934	// §Action Bar and Mutation UX: "describes what changed in plain
   935	// language". Skips fields that are 0 so the typical case reads
   936	// concisely; the Details disclosure carries the full numeric
   937	// breakdown.
   938	function rescanSummary(r: RescanReport): string {
   939	  const parts: string[] = [];
   940	  parts.push(
   941	    `Discovered ${r.discovered_files} ${pluralize(r.discovered_files, "file", "files")}`,
   942	  );
   943	  if (r.parsed_sessions > 0) {
   944	    parts.push(
   945	      `parsed ${r.parsed_sessions} ${pluralize(r.parsed_sessions, "session", "sessions")}`,
   946	    );
   947	  }
   948	  if (r.not_stored_sessions > 0) {
   949	    parts.push(`${r.not_stored_sessions} not yet stored`);
   950	  }
   951	  if (r.outdated_sessions > 0) {
   952	    parts.push(`${r.outdated_sessions} outdated`);
   953	  }
   954	  if (r.scan_errors > 0) {
   955	    parts.push(
   956	      `${r.scan_errors} ${pluralize(r.scan_errors, "scan error", "scan errors")}`,
   957	    );
   958	  }
   959	  return parts.join(", ") + ".";
   960	}
   961	
   962	function rescanCounts(r: RescanReport): import("react").ReactNode {
   963	  return (
   964	    <dl className="toast-counts">
   965	      <dt>discovered_files</dt>
   966	      <dd>{r.discovered_files}</dd>
   967	      <dt>skipped_files</dt>
   968	      <dd>{r.skipped_files}</dd>
   969	      <dt>parsed_sessions</dt>
   970	      <dd>{r.parsed_sessions}</dd>
   971	      <dt>not_stored_sessions</dt>
   972	      <dd>{r.not_stored_sessions}</dd>
   973	      <dt>outdated_sessions</dt>
   974	      <dd>{r.outdated_sessions}</dd>
   975	      <dt>up_to_date_sessions</dt>
   976	      <dd>{r.up_to_date_sessions}</dd>
   977	      <dt>scan_errors</dt>
   978	      <dd>{r.scan_errors}</dd>
   979	    </dl>
   980	  );

     1	// Section-level wrapper for the unified inspection list.
     2	//
     3	// `SessionsView` owns:
     4	//   - rendering the SessionFilters control bar above the table
     5	//   - the per-section error banner when ONE side fetch failed
     6	//     (per-panel error isolation rule carried over from Phase 3 F1:
     7	//     a source 500 must not blank the stored-side rows, and vice
     8	//     versa)
     9	//   - dispatching to one of the four empty-state branches per
    10	//     `working/phase-4.md` §Filter, Sort, Search → Empty States:
    11	//       (1) "No sessions at all" — both fetches resolved to empty
    12	//       (2) "No matches after filter/search" — merged > 0 but
    13	//           filtered === 0 because of an active filter
    14	//       (3) "Nothing to import in the current filter" — filtered > 0
    15	//           but zero importable rows
    16	//       (4) "Partial fetch failure" — one of (source, stored) errored;
    17	//           the per-section banner from M2 still renders + the
    18	//           unified table shows the surviving rows
    19	//   - the M4 detail drawer state (`detailRowKey`) plus the trigger
    20	//     ref. The drawer is always rendered as a sibling of the table
    21	//     (one drawer at a time per spec); `isOpen` controls its modal
    22	//     state. The trigger ref captures the row that opened the
    23	//     drawer so focus can be restored to it on close (the explicit
    24	//     restoration is the path under test in component tests; in
    25	//     real Chromium the platform restoration also fires).
    26	//   - As of M5 the view also renders a `<Pagination>` strip below
    27	//     the table. The strip is a controlled component; `App.tsx`
    28	//     owns the `pageIndex` state (and the `pageSize` field, persisted
    29	//     via `useSessionFilters`). The page-size selector and the
    30	//     prev/next buttons dispatch back through `onChangePage` /
    31	//     `onChangePageSize`. The strip renders even when the page set
    32	//     is empty so the layout doesn't shift.
    33	//
    34	// `App.tsx` retains ownership of fetch state, the merged + filtered
    35	// + paginated row sets (memoized once + passed in), the `selected`
    36	// set, the filter state hook, and every mutation handler.
    37	// SessionsView is presentational except for the drawer state.
    38	//
    39	// Important pagination invariant: `filteredRows` is the FULL
    40	// post-filter set (every page); `pageRows` is the slice for the
    41	// active page. Empty-state branch math reads from `mergedRows` and
    42	// `filteredRows` (NOT `pageRows`) — a paginated list with rows on
    43	// page 2 and an empty page 1 is NOT the same as "no matches after
    44	// filter". The drawer also reads from `filteredRows` so a row that
    45	// is currently off-page is still openable if it's still in the
    46	// filter window (the M4 contract).

    60	export type PanelState<T> =
    61	  | { kind: "loading" }
    62	  | { kind: "ok"; data: T }
    63	  | { kind: "error"; message: string };

    65	export type SessionsViewProps = {
    66	  sourceState: PanelState<SourceSessionView[]>;
    67	  storedState: PanelState<StoredSessionView[]>;
    68	  /** Merged but unfiltered rows. Used to distinguish "no sessions at all"
    69	   *  from "no matches after filter/search". */
    70	  mergedRows: SessionRow[];
    71	  /** Merged + filtered + sorted rows (full set across all pages). The

    75	  /** Active page slice from `applyPagination(filteredRows, pageIndex,

    79	  pageIndex: number;

   118	export function SessionsView({
   119	  sourceState,
   120	  storedState,
   121	  mergedRows,
   122	  filteredRows,
   123	  pageRows,
   124	  pageIndex,
   125	  pageSize,
   126	  onChangePage,
   127	  onChangePageSize,
   128	  filters,

   145	  // Detail drawer state. `detailRowKey === null` -> closed; otherwise

   147	  // stored_only rows must be openable too, so we use the React-level
   148	  // identity that always exists).
   149	  const [detailRowKey, setDetailRowKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
   150	  // Capture the row trigger element so we can restore focus on close.
   151	  // `useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)` lets us pass the same ref into
   152	  // the Drawer's `restoreFocusRef` prop without re-binding on every
   153	  // render.
   154	  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
   155	  // Pick the merged row to render in the drawer body. Read from
   156	  // `filteredRows` (NOT `pageRows`) so the drawer always shows
   157	  // whatever the user can currently see in the filter window, even
   158	  // if the row is on a different page from the one currently
   159	  // displayed. This mirrors the M3 selection model (which is also
   160	  // filter-window scoped, not page-window scoped).
   161	  const selectedDetailRow: SessionRow | null =
   162	    detailRowKey === null
   163	      ? null
   164	      : filteredRows.find((r) => r.rowKey === detailRowKey) ?? null;
   165

   167	    rowKey: string,
   168	    triggerEl: HTMLElement | null,
   169	  ) => {
   170	    triggerRef.current = triggerEl;
   171	    setDetailRowKey(rowKey);
   172	  };
   173	  const handleCloseDetail = () => {
   174	    setDetailRowKey(null);
   175	  };
   176	  // Both sides still loading: render a single "loading" hint. This
   177	  // mirrors the Phase 3 PanelBody behavior so the user sees feedback
   178	  // during the initial fetch.
   179	  if (sourceState.kind === "loading" && storedState.kind === "loading") {
   180	    return <p>Loading sessions...</p>;
   181	  }
   182	
   183	  // Both sides errored: full-section "no sessions could be loaded"

   187	  if (sourceState.kind === "error" && storedState.kind === "error") {
   188	    return (
   189	      <div className="empty">

   193	        </p>
   194	        <p>
   195	          <button type="button" onClick={onRetry}>
   196	            Retry
   197	          </button>
   198	        </p>
   199	      </div>
   200	    );
   201	  }
   202	
   203	  // Decide which empty-state branch (if any) fires for the current
   204	  // (mergedRows, filteredRows) tuple. The branches are mutually
   205	  // exclusive; falling through means we render the table.
   206	  //
   207	  // NOTE: empty-state math reads from `filteredRows`, not `pageRows`.
   208	  // A 100-row filter that pages to "page 2 with 0 items because the
   209	  // user's stale pageIndex landed past the last page" still has
   210	  // matching rows; the pagination clamp in App.tsx fixes the index

   212	  let emptyState: EmptyStateKind | null = null;
   213	  if (mergedRows.length === 0) {
   214	    emptyState = "no_sessions_at_all";
   215	  } else if (filteredRows.length === 0) {
   216	    emptyState = "no_matches_after_filter";
   217	  } else if (filteredRows.every((r) => !isImportable(r))) {
   218	    emptyState = "nothing_to_import";
   219	  }
   220

   223	      {sourceState.kind === "error" ? (
   224	        <p role="alert">

   228	          </button>
   229	        </p>
   230	      ) : null}

   232	        <p role="alert">

   234	          <button type="button" onClick={onRetry}>
   235	            Retry
   236	          </button>
   237	        </p>
   238	      ) : null}
   239	      <SessionFilters
   240	        filters={filters}
   241	        projects={projects}
   242	        setFilter={setFilter}
   243	        setImportableOnly={setImportableOnly}
   244	      />
   245	      {emptyState === "no_sessions_at_all" ? (
   246	        <div className="empty">
   247	          <p>No sessions have been discovered or stored yet.</p>
   248	          <p>
   249	            <button
   250	              type="button"
   251	              onClick={onRescan}
   252	              disabled={rescanPending}
   253	            >
   254	              {rescanPending ? "Rescanning..." : "Rescan"}
   255	            </button>
   256	          </p>
   257	        </div>
   258	      ) : null}
   259	      {emptyState === "no_matches_after_filter" ? (
   260	        <div className="empty">
   261	          <p>No sessions match the current filter.</p>
   262	          <p>
   263	            <button type="button" onClick={resetAll}>
   264	              Clear filters
   265	            </button>
   266	          </p>
   267	        </div>
   268	      ) : null}
   269	      {emptyState === "nothing_to_import" ? (
   270	        <div className="empty">
   271	          <p>Nothing to import in the current filter.</p>
   272	          <p>
   273	            <button
   274	              type="button"
   275	              onClick={() => setImportableOnly(true)}
   276	            >
   277	              Show importable only
   278	            </button>
   279	          </p>
   280	        </div>
   281	      ) : null}
   282	      {/* Render the table when filteredRows > 0, regardless of
   283	          whether the "Nothing to import" empty state is also up.
   284	          The table is informative (rows describe what the user CAN
   285	          see) and the empty-state copy + affordance complement it
   286	          rather than replace it. The two table-suppressing branches
   287	          ("no_sessions_at_all" and "no_matches_after_filter") only
   288	          fire when filteredRows.length === 0, so the table never
   289	          renders zero rows in those branches.
   290	
   291	          The table receives `pageRows` (NOT `filteredRows`) — the
   292	          slice math lives in `applyPagination` and is clamped per
   293	          render so a stale `pageIndex` from React state never
   294	          renders an empty page when filteredRows is non-empty. */}
   295	      {filteredRows.length > 0 ? (
   296	        <>
   297	          <SessionsTable
   298	            rows={pageRows}
   299	            selected={selected}
   300	            onToggle={onToggle}
   301	            onToggleAll={onToggleAll}
   302	            now={now}
   303	            onOpenDetail={handleOpenDetail}
   304	            selectedRowKey={selectedRowKey ?? null}
   305	            onSelectRow={onSelectRow}
   306	            pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey={pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey ?? null}
   307	            onDeepLinkPulseEnd={onDeepLinkPulseEnd}
   308	          />
   309	          <Pagination
   310	            pageSize={pageSize}
   311	            pageIndex={pageIndex}
   312	            totalRows={filteredRows.length}
   313	            onChangePage={onChangePage}
   314	            onChangePageSize={onChangePageSize}
   315	          />
   316	        </>
   317	      ) : null}
   318	      {/* Drawer is ALWAYS rendered — `isOpen` controls the platform
   319	          modal state. A conditional unmount would break the
   320	          showModal()/close() lifecycle and lose the dialog ref between
   321	          opens. */}
   322	      <Drawer
   323	        isOpen={detailRowKey !== null}
   324	        onClose={handleCloseDetail}
   325	        restoreFocusRef={triggerRef}
   326	        ariaLabel={
   327	          selectedDetailRow !== null
   328	            ? `Session detail: ${selectedDetailRow.title ?? "(untitled)"}`
   329	            : "Session detail"
   330	        }
   331	      >
   332	        {selectedDetailRow !== null ? (
   333	          <SessionDetail row={selectedDetailRow} now={now} />
   334	        ) : null}
   335	      </Drawer>
   336	    </>
   337	  );
   338	}
   339	
   340	type EmptyStateKind =
   341	  | "no_sessions_at_all"
   342	  | "no_matches_after_filter"
   343	  | "nothing_to_import";

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Presentational table for the unified inspection list.
     2	//
     3	// Receives the merged + filtered + sorted `SessionRow[]` plus the
     4	// `selected` set + toggle handlers from the parent. Selection keys are
     5	// always backend-provided `sourceSessionKey` strings — never the
     6	// React-only `rowKey` fallback for `stored_only` rows. The table
     7	// renders a checkbox ONLY on importable rows
     8	// (`isImportable(row) === true`) so non-importable rows cannot enter
     9	// the import POST.
    10	//
    11	// Per `working/phase-4.md` §Action Bar and Mutation UX, the bulk-select
    12	// affordance toggles ALL importable rows in the current filter window.
    13	// Non-importable rows are invisible to the bulk action.
    14	//
    15	// Per spec §Data Model in the Browser, a row whose `statusConflict` is
    16	// true gets a small "(refresh)" affordance next to its inline status
    17	// badge — the M2 minimum for the "fetched state changed during load —
    18	// refresh" hint. The drawer header in M4 ALSO gets its own "Conflict"
    19	// badge; per the open risk in `progress/phase-4.progress.md` the
    20	// row-side affordance MUST stay (the drawer-side one supplements
    21	// rather than replaces it).
    22	//
    23	// Per spec, a row whose `sourcePathIsStale` is true labels its
    24	// source-path cell with a `title=` hover hint clarifying that the
    25	// path is the last-known location, not currently discoverable.
    26	//
    27	// As of M3 the "Updated" cell renders a relative-time string against
    28	// a single `now` captured at refetch time in `App.tsx` (passed in as a
    29	// prop) so the page does not ticker-update. The full ISO timestamp
    30	// stays available via the `title=` hover hint for users who need the
    31	// absolute value.
    32	//
    33	// As of M4 (Chunk E1) the row itself becomes the drawer trigger: a
    34	// click anywhere on the row OR pressing Enter while the row is
    35	// focused calls `onOpenDetail(row.rowKey, triggerEl)`. The trigger
    36	// element is forwarded so the parent can stash it in a ref and
    37	// restore focus to it after the drawer closes (happy-dom does not
    38	// implement the platform focus-restoration on `dialog.close()`; real
    39	// Chromium does, but the explicit ref makes the behaviour
    40	// deterministic across both targets). The checkbox cell stops
    41	// propagation so toggling selection never opens the drawer (a11y bug
    42	// magnet). `onOpenDetail` is optional with a no-op default for
    43	// backward compatibility with M2/M3 tests that did not pass it.
    44	//
    45	// As of M6 (Chunk G) the status pill is rendered inline (the dedicated
    46	// `StatusBadge` component was retired). The transform —
    47	// `variant = status.replace(/_/g, "-")` for the CSS class and
    48	// `label = status.replace(/_/g, " ")` for the visible text — is
    49	// preserved byte-for-byte at the call site so the DOM shape stays
    50	// `<span class="badge {variant}">{label}</span>`.
    51	//
    52	// CSS lives in the sibling `SessionsTable.css` (selectors
    53	// `.table-wrap`, table chrome, `.badge.*`, `.raw-link`, `.select-col`).
    54	// Global utility classes (`.muted`, `.mono`, `.stack`, `.empty`) live
    55	// in `styles/global.css`; the filter-bar CSS is in
    56	// `SessionFilters.css`.
    57	import { relativeTimeFrom } from "./relativeTime";
    58	import { isImportable, type SessionRow } from "./types";
    59	import "./SessionsTable.css";
    60	
    61	export type SessionsTableProps = {
    62	  rows: SessionRow[];
    63	  /** Backend-provided `sourceSessionKey` values currently selected. */
    64	  selected: Set<string>;
    65	  /** Toggle a single importable row by its `sourceSessionKey`. */
    66	  onToggle: (sourceSessionKey: string) => void;
    67	  /** Toggle all importable rows: if any importable row is unchecked, select all; otherwise clear. */
    68	  onToggleAll: () => void;
    69	  /** Pinned-`now` ISO string used by the relative-time cell renderer. */
    70	  now: string;
    71	  /** Open the detail drawer for `rowKey`. The second argument is the
    72	   *  trigger DOM element so the parent can stash it in a ref and
    73	   *  restore focus on close. Optional for backward compatibility with
    74	   *  M2/M3 callers that don't render a drawer. Defaults to a no-op. */
    75	  onOpenDetail?: (rowKey: string, triggerEl: HTMLElement | null) => void;
    76	  /** M1a: rowKey of the currently URL-selected session. The matched
    77	   *  row carries `aria-current="true"` for assistive tech and the
    78	   *  selected-row visual treatment. `null` means no selection. */
    79	  selectedRowKey?: string | null;
    80	  /** M1a: setter for the URL-synced selection. Called on row click
    81	   *  (NOT on checkbox-cell click — the importability rule's
    82	   *  stopPropagation guard remains load-bearing). The Phase-4
    83	   *  `onOpenDetail` callback ALSO fires on the same click so the
    84	   *  drawer flow stays reachable until M1b retires it. */
    85	  onSelectRow?: (rowKey: string) => void;
    86	  /** M1a: rowKey of the row that should fire the deep-link pulse on
    87	   *  the current paint. Set on initial mount only when
    88	   *  `URLSearchParams.get("session")` was non-null; cleared by the
    89	   *  parent via `onAnimationEnd` OR a 2 s safety timer. Click-driven
    90	   *  selection MUST NOT set this — the pulse marks URL-driven
    91	   *  arrivals only (spec line 585 + Resolved Decision #19). */
    92	  pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey?: string | null;
    93	  /** M1a: notify the parent that the deep-link pulse animation has
    94	   *  ended on `rowKey` so the parent can clear
    95	   *  `pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey`. Optional. */
    96	  onDeepLinkPulseEnd?: (rowKey: string) => void;
    97	};
    98	
    99	export function SessionsTable({
   100	  rows,
   101	  selected,
   102	  onToggle,
   103	  onToggleAll,
   104	  now,
   105	  onOpenDetail,
   106	  selectedRowKey = null,
   107	  onSelectRow,
   108	  pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey = null,
   109	  onDeepLinkPulseEnd,
   110	}: SessionsTableProps) {
   111	  if (rows.length === 0) {
   112	    return (
   113	      <div className="empty">
   114	        No sessions have been discovered or stored yet.
   115	      </div>
   116	    );
   117	  }
   118	  // Header bulk-select state is computed from the importable subset
   119	  // only. A row that cannot be selected (no checkbox) does not
   120	  // participate in "all selected" / "indeterminate" math.
   121	  const importableKeys: string[] = [];
   122	  for (const row of rows) {
   123	    if (isImportable(row) && row.sourceSessionKey !== null) {
   124	      importableKeys.push(row.sourceSessionKey);
   125	    }
   126	  }
   127	  const importableSelectedCount = importableKeys.reduce(
   128	    (acc, k) => (selected.has(k) ? acc + 1 : acc),
   129	    0,
   130	  );
   131	  const allChecked =
   132	    importableKeys.length > 0 &&
   133	    importableSelectedCount === importableKeys.length;
   134	  const someChecked =
   135	    importableSelectedCount > 0 &&
   136	    importableSelectedCount < importableKeys.length;
   137	  // When zero rows are importable we still render the header checkbox
   138	  // but it stays disabled — there's nothing to bulk-select.
   139	  const headerCheckboxDisabled = importableKeys.length === 0;
   140	
   141	  return (
   142	    <div className="table-wrap">
   143	      <table>
   144	        <thead>
   145	          <tr>
   146	            <th className="select-col">
   147	              <input
   148	                type="checkbox"
   149	                aria-label="Select all importable sessions"
   150	                checked={allChecked}
   151	                disabled={headerCheckboxDisabled}
   152	                ref={(el) => {
   153	                  if (el) el.indeterminate = someChecked;
   154	                }}
   155	                onChange={onToggleAll}
   156	              />
   157	            </th>
   158	            <th>Status</th>
   159	            <th>Tool</th>
   160	            <th>Title</th>
   161	            <th>Project</th>
   162	            <th>Updated</th>
   163	            <th>Stored Copy</th>
   164	            <th>Source Path</th>
   165	          </tr>
   166	        </thead>
   167	        <tbody>
   168	          {rows.map((row) => {
   169	            const importable = isImportable(row);
   170	            const checked =
   171	              row.sourceSessionKey !== null &&
   172	              selected.has(row.sourceSessionKey);
   173	            // Stored-copy display: stored UID + ingested-at when present;
   174	            // otherwise the "not stored" sentinel.
   175	            const storedUidLine =
   176	              row.storedSessionUid !== null
   177	                ? row.storedSessionUid
   178	                : "not stored";
   179	            const ingestedLine = `ingested: ${row.ingestedAt ?? "—"}`;
   180	            // The View Raw anchor is rendered next to the stored UID
   181	            // when there is a stored copy — it's the M2 carry-over from
   182	            // the dual-table layout's stored-side anchor.
   183	            const rawLinkHref =
   184	              row.storedSessionUid !== null
   185	                ? `/api/v1/sessions/${row.storedSessionUid}/raw`
   186	                : null;
   187	            const metadataHref =
   188	              row.storedSessionUid !== null
   189	                ? `/api/v1/sessions/${row.storedSessionUid}`
   190	                : null;
   191	            const handleRowOpen = (
   192	              triggerEl: HTMLElement | null,
   193	            ) => {
   194	              // M1a: a row "open" gesture has TWO side effects:
   195	              //   1. Phase-4 `onOpenDetail` (still mounts the drawer
   196	              //      so the focus-trap walk + e2e steps stay green
   197	              //      until M1b retires this).
   198	              //   2. M1a `onSelectRow` (drives URL-synced selection
   199	              //      via the App-level useSelectedSession hook so
   200	              //      the right pane swaps to ready-placeholder).
   201	              // The two are intentional duplicates during M1a; M1b
   202	              // will retire the drawer flow and leave `onSelectRow`
   203	              // alone.
   204	              if (onOpenDetail) onOpenDetail(row.rowKey, triggerEl);
   205	              if (onSelectRow) onSelectRow(row.rowKey);
   206	            };
   207	            const isSelected = selectedRowKey === row.rowKey;
   208	            const isPulseTarget =
   209	              pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey === row.rowKey;
   210	            return (
   211	              <tr
   212	                key={row.rowKey}
   213	                tabIndex={0}
   214	                aria-current={isSelected ? "true" : undefined}
   215	                data-deep-link={isPulseTarget ? "true" : undefined}
   216	                onAnimationEnd={
   217	                  isPulseTarget && onDeepLinkPulseEnd
   218	                    ? () => onDeepLinkPulseEnd(row.rowKey)
   219	                    : undefined
   220	                }
   221	                onClick={(event) => {
   222	                  // Walk up from the click target to see whether the
   223	                  // event came from inside the checkbox column. A
   224	                  // click on the checkbox cell (or anything inside
   225	                  // it) toggles selection only — it MUST NOT open
   226	                  // the drawer (a11y bug magnet). The
   227	                  // `event.stopPropagation()` on the cell handles
   228	                  // most cases; this guard is the belt-and-braces
   229	                  // backup in case the propagation interception is
   230	                  // bypassed by an event-time bubble re-fire.
   231	                  const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
   232	                  if (target?.closest("td.select-col") !== null) return;
   233	                  handleRowOpen(event.currentTarget);
   234	                }}
   235	                onKeyDown={(event) => {
   236	                  if (event.key !== "Enter") return;
   237	                  // Same checkbox-cell guard as the click handler.
   238	                  const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
   239	                  if (target?.closest("td.select-col") !== null) return;
   240	                  // Prevent the default form-submission behaviour
   241	                  // (no form here, but Enter on a focused element
   242	                  // can still trigger native actions in some
   243	                  // contexts).
   244	                  event.preventDefault();
   245	                  handleRowOpen(event.currentTarget);
   246	                }}
   247	              >
   248	                <td
   249	                  className="select-col"
   250	                  // Stop click propagation BEFORE the row's onClick
   251	                  // sees it — toggling selection should never open
   252	                  // the drawer.
   253	                  onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
   254	                >
   255	                  {importable && row.sourceSessionKey !== null ? (
   256	                    <input
   257	                      type="checkbox"
   258	                      aria-label={`Select ${row.sourceSessionKey}`}
   259	                      checked={checked}
   260	                      onChange={() => onToggle(row.sourceSessionKey!)}
   261	                    />
   262	                  ) : null}
   263	                </td>
   264	                <td>
   265	                  <span
   266	                    className={`badge ${row.status.replace(/_/g, "-")}`}
   267	                  >
   268	                    {row.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   269	                  </span>
   270	                  {row.statusConflict ? (
   271	                    <>
   272	                      {" "}
   273	                      <span
   274	                        className="muted"
   275	                        title="Source and stored status disagreed during load — refresh to re-fetch."
   276	                      >
   277	                        (refresh)
   278	                      </span>
   279	                    </>
   280	                  ) : null}
   281	                </td>
   282	                <td className="mono">{row.tool}</td>
   283	                <td className="stack">
   284	                  <strong>{row.title ?? "(untitled)"}</strong>
   285	                  <span className="muted mono">{row.rowKey}</span>
   286	                </td>
   287	                <td>{row.projectPath ?? "—"}</td>
   288	                <td
   289	                  className="mono"
   290	                  title={row.sourceUpdatedAt ?? undefined}
   291	                >
   292	                  {relativeTimeFrom(now, row.sourceUpdatedAt)}
   293	                </td>
   294	                <td className="stack">
   295	                  {metadataHref !== null ? (
   296	                    <a className="raw-link mono" href={metadataHref}>
   297	                      {storedUidLine}
   298	                    </a>
   299	                  ) : (
   300	                    <span className="mono muted">{storedUidLine}</span>
   301	                  )}
   302	                  <span className="muted mono">{ingestedLine}</span>
   303	                  {rawLinkHref !== null ? (
   304	                    <a className="raw-link" href={rawLinkHref}>
   305	                      View Raw
   306	                    </a>
   307	                  ) : null}
   308	                </td>
   309	                <td
   310	                  className="mono"
   311	                  title={
   312	                    row.sourcePathIsStale
   313	                      ? "last seen source path — source file no longer discoverable"
   314	                      : undefined
   315	                  }
   316	                >
   317	                  {row.sourcePath}
   318	                </td>
   319	              </tr>
   320	            );
   321	          })}
   322	        </tbody>
   323	      </table>
   324	    </div>
   325	  );
   326	}

codex

+  // hook owns the React state + the History API mirror; App.tsx
+  // owns every consumer that needs to know about the current
+  // selection. Per Resolved Decision #20 (spec line 1165), selection
+  // ownership lives in App.tsx (not in SessionsView).

+
+  // M1a: stacked-narrow viewport mode. The two panes stay React-
+  // mounted regardless of mode; CSS-attribute selectors on
+  // `<main>[data-narrow-mode="..."]` drive visibility. Default is
+  // "list" — on narrow viewports the user lands on the list and a
+  // row click promotes them to "session". On wide viewports
+  // (>= 900 px) the value is meaningless because both panes always
+  // render; we still keep state synced so resize → narrow doesn't
+  // surprise the user with a flipped mode.
+  const [narrowMode, setNarrowMode] =
+    useState<"list" | "session">(() =>
+      // If the URL pre-selected a row on mount, the user is
+      // expecting to see the right pane on first paint. Default
+      // narrowMode to "session" in that case so a narrow-viewport
+      // deep-link arrival shows the session pane immediately
+      // (matches the M1a design.md §5.3 / spec lines 597–611).
+      selectedRowKey !== null ? "session" : "list",
+    );

+  // on initial mount, write the same row key into a separate slice
+  // so the matched list row applies `data-deep-link="true"` for one
+  // paint cycle. The cleanup path is "whichever fires first":
+  // either the row's `onAnimationEnd` clears it, or a 2 s safety
+  // timer clears it unconditionally (the row may never render in
+  // the session_not_found case). Click-driven selection NEVER fires
+  // the pulse (spec line 585) — that's why the pulse target is
+  // captured ONCE on initial mount, not whenever selectedRowKey
+  // changes.

+  ] = useState<string | null>(() => selectedRowKey);

+  // animation-end path or from unmount.
+  const pulseTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
+  useEffect(() => {
+    // Only start the safety timer if there's a pulse target on
+    // initial mount. Subsequent state changes do not re-arm the
+    // pulse — the pulse mechanism is a one-shot per page load,
+    // matched against the URL on initial mount.
+    if (pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey === null) return;
+    pulseTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
+      setPendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey(null);
+      pulseTimerRef.current = null;
+    }, 2000);
+    return () => {
+      if (pulseTimerRef.current !== null) {
+        clearTimeout(pulseTimerRef.current);
+        pulseTimerRef.current = null;
+      }
+    };
+    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
+  }, []); // mount-only; pulse target is captured at initial render
+  const onDeepLinkPulseEnd = useCallback((rowKey: string) => {
+    setPendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey((prev) =>
+      prev === rowKey ? null : prev,
+    );
+    if (pulseTimerRef.current !== null) {
+      clearTimeout(pulseTimerRef.current);
+      pulseTimerRef.current = null;
+    }
+  }, []);

+  // M1a: Clear selection from the session_not_found state.
+  // Identical to Esc's effect on the URL: clears selectedRowKey
+  // and removes the `?session=` query param. Does NOT clear
+  // narrowMode (the user is on a narrow viewport ⇒ already
+  // visible-mode "session" but now empty; we flip to "list" so they
+  // see the list).
+  const handleClearSelectionFromNotFound = useCallback(() => {
+    selectRow(null);
+    setNarrowMode("list");
+  }, [selectRow]);
+
+  // M1a: global `Esc` handler. Clears the URL `?session=` slice +
+  // selectedRowKey + narrowMode in a single keystroke. Per spec
+  // line 567 + Resolved Decision #17, the handler is SCOPED:
+  // ignored when focus is in `<input>`, `<textarea>`,
+  // `[contenteditable]`, or any `[role="combobox"]`. This is so the
+  // Phase-4 SessionFilters search input still consumes Esc to
+  // clear its own value (the existing UX); only Esc events that
+  // bubble out of non-editable surfaces reach this handler.
+  //
+  // Identical behavior across narrow + wide viewports — on narrow
+  // it ALSO resets narrowMode to "list" (no session pane to be in).
+  useEffect(() => {
+    function isEditableTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
+      if (target === null) return false;
+      const el = target as HTMLElement;
+      // Tag check: input / textarea — both directly consume Esc
+      // semantics in their default behavior.
+      const tag = (el.tagName ?? "").toUpperCase();
+      if (tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA") return true;
+      // contenteditable elements (rare in this app, but the spec
+      // calls them out so M2 surfaces such as inline-edit notes
+      // would already be covered).
+      if (
+        typeof el.closest === "function" &&
+        el.closest('[contenteditable="true"]') !== null
+      ) {
+        return true;
+      }
+      // ARIA combobox role — covers e.g. the future-typeahead
+      // autocomplete patterns.
+      if (
+        typeof el.closest === "function" &&
+        el.closest('[role="combobox"]') !== null
+      ) {
+        return true;
+      }
+      return false;
+    }
+    function onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
+      if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
+      if (isEditableTarget(event.target)) return;
+      // setSelectedRowKey(null) + URL clear + narrowMode reset.
+      // selectRow handles the first two; setNarrowMode handles the
+      // third. All three are idempotent so calling them when there
+      // is nothing to clear is a no-op.
+      selectRow(null);
+      setNarrowMode("list");
+    }
+    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
+    return () => {
+      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
+    };
+  }, [selectRow]);
+
+  // M1a: derive the right-pane state. The four-state machine
+  // (per design.md §4.1) maps the (selectedRowKey, mergedRows,
+  // panel-state) tuple to one of:
+  //   - empty               → no selection
+  //   - loading             → selection set; row not yet merged;
+  //                           any GET still in flight
+  //   - ready-placeholder   → selection set; row IS in merged set
+  //                           (M1a placeholder until M2's tabs)
+  //   - session_not_found   → selection set; row NOT in merged set;
+  //                           ALL three GETs settled (per spec line
+  //                           572 — the "all three settled" gate is
+  //                           load-bearing because resolving "missing
+  //                           row" too early would flash spurious
+  //                           copy while fetches are still in flight)
+  // Note: `buildUrl` is referenced just to keep it in the import
+  // graph for future call sites (Esc + selectRow already use it via
+  // the hook).
+  void buildUrl; // see comment above
+  let sessionViewState:
+    | "empty"
+    | "loading"
+    | "ready-placeholder"
+    | "session_not_found";
+  if (selectedRowKey === null) {
+    sessionViewState = "empty";
+  } else {
+    const matchedRow = mergedRows.find((r) => r.rowKey === selectedRowKey);
+    if (matchedRow !== undefined) {
+      sessionViewState = "ready-placeholder";
+    } else if (
+      sourceState.kind === "loading" ||
+      storedState.kind === "loading" ||
+      errorsState.kind === "loading"
+    ) {
+      // While any of the three GETs is still in flight, render the
+      // generic "loading" copy. Resolving "missing row" too early
+      // would flash session_not_found while the merged list is
+      // still settling.
+      sessionViewState = "loading";
+    } else {
+      sessionViewState = "session_not_found";
+    }
+  }
+
+  // M1a: detect the narrow viewport so we can decide whether the
+  // "← Back to list" affordance should render. The CSS-driven
+  // `display: none` rules already hide the wrong pane below 900 px;
+  // this gate just keeps the back-to-list button DOM-present only
+  // on narrow + session mode (avoids a wide-viewport user finding
+  // it via screen reader or aria-* tooling).
+  const [isNarrow, setIsNarrow] = useState<boolean>(() => {
+    if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
+    if (typeof window.matchMedia !== "function") return false;
+    return window.matchMedia("(max-width: 899.98px)").matches;
+  });
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
+    if (typeof window.matchMedia !== "function") return;
+    const mq = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 899.98px)");
+    const onChange = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => setIsNarrow(e.matches);
+    // addEventListener is the modern API; addListener is the legacy
+    // fallback. happy-dom + Chromium ship the former.
+    if (typeof mq.addEventListener === "function") {
+      mq.addEventListener("change", onChange);
+    } else if (typeof (mq as unknown as { addListener?: typeof onChange }).addListener === "function") {
+      (mq as unknown as { addListener: (l: typeof onChange) => void }).addListener(onChange);
+    }
+    return () => {
+      if (typeof mq.removeEventListener === "function") {
+        mq.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
+      } else if (
+        typeof (mq as unknown as { removeListener?: typeof onChange }).removeListener === "function"
+      ) {
+        (mq as unknown as { removeListener: (l: typeof onChange) => void }).removeListener(onChange);
+      }
+    };
+  }, []);
+  const showBackToList = isNarrow && narrowMode === "session";
+
   return (
     <>
-      <main>
-        <h1>Distill Portal</h1>
-        <section className="panel">
-          <h2>Sessions</h2>
-          <ActionBar

-            pending={pending}

-            onClearSelection={onClearSelection}
-            lastRescanAt={lastRescanAt}
-            now={now}
-          />
-          <SessionsView
-            sourceState={sourceState}

-            onChangePage={onChangePage}
-            onChangePageSize={onChangePageSize}
-            filters={filters}
-            projects={projects}
-            setFilter={setFilter}
-            setImportableOnly={setImportableOnly}
-            resetAll={resetAll}
-            selected={selected}
-            onToggle={handleToggle}
-            onToggleAll={handleToggleAll}
-            onRetry={() => {
-              void refetchAll();
-            }}
-            onRescan={handleRescan}
-            rescanPending={pending === "rescan"}
-            now={now}
-          />
-        </section>
-        {errorsState.kind === "error" ? (
-          <p role="alert">
-            Failed to load scan errors: {errorsState.message}{" "}
-            <button type="button" onClick={() => void refetchAll()}>

-            </button>
-          </p>
-        ) : (
-          <ScanErrorsCallout
-            errors={errorsState.kind === "ok" ? errorsState.data : []}

-        )}
+      {/* M1a: page title + scan-errors callout sit above the split-

+          single side. The toast queue stays a sibling of <main>

+    //         the row merges in it should be "ready-placeholder")
+    //       - the matched list row carries either
+    //         data-deep-link="true" briefly (during the 600 ms
+    //         pulse) OR aria-current="true" (after the pulse settles)
+    //       - reload (page.reload) preserves the URL state and the
+    //         right-pane mount: the same row remains aria-current
+    //         after the reload.
+    //
+    //     Note: the existing drawer flow (steps 9 + 10) ALSO opens
+    //     when the user clicks a row — that's the M1a "Phase 4
+    //     drawer flow preserved" invariant. M1b will retire it.
+    //     Here we exercise the SEPARATE URL-driven path; the drawer
+    //     does NOT open from a deep-link arrival because no click
+    //     happens.
+    await page.goto(`/?session=${encodeURIComponent(FIXTURE_SESSION_KEY)}`);
+    await expect(
+      page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Distill Portal", level: 1 }),
+    ).toBeVisible();
+    // <article class="session-pane"> is in the DOM with a
+    // data-state attribute.
+    const sessionPane = page.locator("article.session-pane");
+    await expect(sessionPane).toHaveCount(1);
+    // Once the row merges in, the data-state flips to
+    // "ready-placeholder". (M2 will swap this for the four-tab
+    // strip; M1a only renders the placeholder copy.)
+    await expect(sessionPane).toHaveAttribute(
+      "data-state",
+      "ready-placeholder",
+      { timeout: 5_000 },
+    );
+    // The matched row carries aria-current="true" after the pulse
+    // settles. We assert aria-current rather than data-deep-link
+    // because the pulse is timing-sensitive (600 ms ease-out plus
+    // the 2 s safety timer); aria-current is the stable post-pulse
+    // attribute.
+    const ariaCurrentRow = page.locator(`tr[aria-current="true"]`);
+    await expect(ariaCurrentRow).toBeVisible({ timeout: 5_000 });
+    await expect(ariaCurrentRow).toContainText(FIXTURE_SESSION_KEY);
+
+    // Reload the page; the URL state must survive and the same
+    // row must remain selected.
+    await page.reload();
+    await expect(sessionPane).toHaveAttribute(
+      "data-state",
+      "ready-placeholder",
+      { timeout: 5_000 },
+    );
+    await expect(
+      page.locator(`tr[aria-current="true"]`),
+    ).toContainText(FIXTURE_SESSION_KEY, { timeout: 5_000 });
   });
